# Changelog

All notable changes to ProtoCore are documented here.

## [Unreleased]

### Bug Fixes

- SonarCloud bugs and vulnerabilities on main ([`a68727d`](https://github.com/dstroy0/ProtoCore/commit/a68727dea2d1b0c2a3685a60228a98343c7659d7))
- an inbound accept could take the SSH slot the reverse tunnel held ([`c4e2dd6`](https://github.com/dstroy0/ProtoCore/commit/c4e2dd6dfe3ce1cc3779cbee1205c0f92e37c7b6))
- the client built its own KEXINIT, and I_C / I_S were sized for one role ([`91ded0d`](https://github.com/dstroy0/ProtoCore/commit/91ded0d9af6f1fd21ef17f998af31c5a76fc7a4b))
- the client role could not complete a key exchange ([`feea9cb`](https://github.com/dstroy0/ProtoCore/commit/feea9cbdccfbe507334264514efb3a1d5d38115d))
- ssh_scp.c never compiled, name the mode enum as the header declares it ([`b7c9a78`](https://github.com/dstroy0/ProtoCore/commit/b7c9a78b9d529f427ed633019a38e81114772519))
- ssh_forward.c never compiled, add the missing session.h include ([`3b2cd80`](https://github.com/dstroy0/ProtoCore/commit/3b2cd80b487f802884ec49773de99c7f2733037c))
- the software server was the fourth site accepting an all-zero secret ([`6f9c6c3`](https://github.com/dstroy0/ProtoCore/commit/6f9c6c3ac88f1321ad6053a230d3f4e88e5f4796))
- RFC 8446 7.4.2 requires aborting on an all-zero X25519 shared secret ([`d19904d`](https://github.com/dstroy0/ProtoCore/commit/d19904d92b6464b583453d9048703ba2eb1adc79))
- the curator's cap filled with flagged-valid entries, shipping no rejection cases ([`dbe4faa`](https://github.com/dstroy0/ProtoCore/commit/dbe4faa97f06fe84cffbdeecc437fc3625df69d1))
- protocore_config.h is the entry point, not types.h ([`96598a7`](https://github.com/dstroy0/ProtoCore/commit/96598a7c1096ff5f0783e4bb1e3bfa9b49070cc8))
- three headers used the macro without the header that defines it ([`9662510`](https://github.com/dstroy0/ProtoCore/commit/9662510c4dcb333b022c1a602e52f352024fed79))
- every header carries its own extern C; protocore.h stops wrapping its includes ([`3bb3693`](https://github.com/dstroy0/ProtoCore/commit/3bb36938c436d8821d3fcfb80db00edf51bdf293))
- the exchange hash declared a 64-byte out its SHA-256 caller does not have ([`28b6987`](https://github.com/dstroy0/ProtoCore/commit/28b698788ceb76acbdd8e7584d12e472840f61bc))
- the Ns table made every leaf an indirect always_inline call at -Og ([`91d646e`](https://github.com/dstroy0/ProtoCore/commit/91d646e82c3f2787c2a30915f9ab08bf738412ba))
- the i2c command link was shared per bus; pc_mnt_fs published a C++ type ([`92089b3`](https://github.com/dstroy0/ProtoCore/commit/92089b348e2ec9037b6f2df6923fea778ecdb2e2))
- the nvs seam serialises; seven fs entry points and one spi transfer were unbounded ([`6a02fe1`](https://github.com/dstroy0/ProtoCore/commit/6a02fe171172732c2e3e6716a14ada4baa4e69c5))
- put_blob and put_str judged their bounds after committing, or not at all ([`8f9ffbe`](https://github.com/dstroy0/ProtoCore/commit/8f9ffbe798f6f8dc2516ccf524abb80a8e1113e2))
- the two backends gave different wrong answers for a blob and a string ([`67924b4`](https://github.com/dstroy0/ProtoCore/commit/67924b4be40529e9704bf2ed07345dc0fd71ff05))
- the fs backend had no sync, so the WAL could not bind the only persistent store ([`945d590`](https://github.com/dstroy0/ProtoCore/commit/945d5906bcf57d1aa1b43c903f64e5f89d2a4dcb))
- a conflicted autostash pop committed its markers into the changelog ([`4618428`](https://github.com/dstroy0/ProtoCore/commit/46184280dedf657a9fd2ed94580100fc8ee34e18))
- modmul guarded its operand count nowhere; stale .cpp references ([`9722aaf`](https://github.com/dstroy0/ProtoCore/commit/9722aaf63577ce0af32746cfdb76564edc1ed2fc))
- the RSA memory-init wait was inverted; four more the vendor arm never proved ([`4fdedaa`](https://github.com/dstroy0/ProtoCore/commit/4fdedaa220ea42d7f60daaee20beff3e0c0afd9c))
- sha256's accelerated arm dropped the work parameter; the KDF term ignored its context ([`817a2d8`](https://github.com/dstroy0/ProtoCore/commit/817a2d881bc570c8914146cbfd86de5267e94d48))
- a liboqs disagreement read as liboqs being absent; scope the agent rules to tools/ ([`671178b`](https://github.com/dstroy0/ProtoCore/commit/671178b20f4a7e66800f5b603658c2be5a5dc302))
- the vendor arm hashed at an offset only the software arm defined ([`2a5ef96`](https://github.com/dstroy0/ProtoCore/commit/2a5ef96d297ec3af6aceef3bdac8a82eca0a8426))
- the misaligned priming load ran with less than a word of work left ([`5ac71a5`](https://github.com/dstroy0/ProtoCore/commit/5ac71a5c2b96007c8d4da0a4f105d3da038aa747))
- claiming a slot orphaned both engines' borrows ([`7bd459d`](https://github.com/dstroy0/ProtoCore/commit/7bd459d3f3ce7b8f11cc28f53b5598491b6908a0))
- the init wipe ran through the pointer from before the split ([`f8ec193`](https://github.com/dstroy0/ProtoCore/commit/f8ec1938e036ed9d069c58c240dae209509e4e12))
- a re-used stream slot served the previous request's pseudo-headers ([`e522163`](https://github.com/dstroy0/ProtoCore/commit/e522163f8c92df5291c26b2ec92387b9f56b7760))
- the re-init split ran from a base it had already zeroed away ([`8549cfc`](https://github.com/dstroy0/ProtoCore/commit/8549cfcea4e8f8d1dd1fa16e8722c5b1f34789a7))
- an invalid record is discarded, the association survives ([`4da8c8d`](https://github.com/dstroy0/ProtoCore/commit/4da8c8dde3d4f9b09c411bb2e6d36234d139d658))
- legacy_record_version is ignored, per RFC 9147 sec 4 ([`fa816e0`](https://github.com/dstroy0/ProtoCore/commit/fa816e0e012129f6e7fa81532e442fea03f897a9))
- a retransmitted fragment that changes a byte aborts reassembly ([`b42543b`](https://github.com/dstroy0/ProtoCore/commit/b42543b0c2f19d6bb581472b337bfb477aed78d9))
- illegal_parameter for a bad cookie, abort a non-empty legacy_cookie, and RFC 7250 outcome 2 ([`2f97cd9`](https://github.com/dstroy0/ProtoCore/commit/2f97cd944e7dee230a11151a68816ed7845ee8f1))
- the key schedule reaches its own region by field, like the rest ([`cefbb5a`](https://github.com/dstroy0/ProtoCore/commit/cefbb5a7b210e9df86946b227f3f828631d789ef))
- reach the connection's own storage, like ssh_pkt_slot_storage ([`90c013e`](https://github.com/dstroy0/ProtoCore/commit/90c013ed957b7cf1bb31c9c6e84a9199058248d7))
- the slot picks its region of the pool's one borrow ([`057c4eb`](https://github.com/dstroy0/ProtoCore/commit/057c4ebd3050084320b2c95aaea1c894a8262d3d))
- the borrow is taken once per connection, not once per init ([`cca0cc8`](https://github.com/dstroy0/ProtoCore/commit/cca0cc8abd613b3c096e84de8ce7021cec635816))
- the portable TLS arm is a third carrier of PC_ENABLE_TLS_RPK ([`3a50d26`](https://github.com/dstroy0/ProtoCore/commit/3a50d267a1aa9443305af7c99bda41d1941973c6))
- a request past 255*HashLen has no defined answer (RFC 5869 sec 2.3) ([`f4de8e4`](https://github.com/dstroy0/ProtoCore/commit/f4de8e410c03d3b75a04ee673314a950c017a5dc))
- the suite check uses the IANA code point constant ([`b317721`](https://github.com/dstroy0/ProtoCore/commit/b3177217d0b9cc33c0ea9b1d2c8978ed6fe0b0e4))
- the offered cipher suite and legacy_compression_methods are checked ([`a5d482c`](https://github.com/dstroy0/ProtoCore/commit/a5d482c21e586f7f469549e885671608d94f6c12))
- a zero-length Handshake or Alert record is neither sent nor accepted ([`de4cb1e`](https://github.com/dstroy0/ProtoCore/commit/de4cb1eeb43627e2cb9668761afeaae9812ee3b1))
- h3_fail is defined above its first use ([`5dd9916`](https://github.com/dstroy0/ProtoCore/commit/5dd9916cdf46903950ef7feb0e8e5574f7664f45))
- Fixed Bit, Reserved Bits and the 2^60 max_streams bound (RFC 9000) ([`518047e`](https://github.com/dstroy0/ProtoCore/commit/518047e2d718ca29d29e62d4f667388dc849b853))
- a content-length that disagrees with the DATA makes the request malformed ([`f062435`](https://github.com/dstroy0/ProtoCore/commit/f062435d89a7d8e84345e20809d688120c1c7269))
- per-type frame length and stream-id guards, and a CONTINUATION cap ([`823d64b`](https://github.com/dstroy0/ProtoCore/commit/823d64b4b7782a52b8431238b19fae0f8ea0d542))
- coaps_server passed the address of a keys pointer, not the keys ([`5f55a22`](https://github.com/dstroy0/ProtoCore/commit/5f55a224d98d296dff916b7a12fd3b646bad7383))
- a HEADERS on an open stream is a trailer section, not a stream-id error ([`d7b3407`](https://github.com/dstroy0/ProtoCore/commit/d7b34072e3b1d9177a05776e4fa53bf863069d3b))
- h2_server suite passed pc_h2_write_header its arguments out of order ([`764879b`](https://github.com/dstroy0/ProtoCore/commit/764879b91d7fe30170fa10daa73334c69aff4e7a))
- reject a zero or overflowing WINDOW_UPDATE (RFC 9113 sec 6.9, 6.9.1) ([`cc03eb3`](https://github.com/dstroy0/ProtoCore/commit/cc03eb3ebc683bb56e730793e42cbf522db2f634))
- the outbound frame borrows from the plaintext arena HTTP works out of ([`808c334`](https://github.com/dstroy0/ProtoCore/commit/808c33467bf7e6944ec40eecd7f2e2c27a81719e))
- DATA on an idle or closed stream is refused before the app sees it (RFC 9113 sec 5.1, 6.1) ([`71a46a7`](https://github.com/dstroy0/ProtoCore/commit/71a46a790db0a957609c21643cf18771076f9725))
- an oversize dynamic-table size update is a decoding error, not a clamp ([`80dae83`](https://github.com/dstroy0/ProtoCore/commit/80dae839fe5b15016fdc227655bd87d1a9b6de1e))
- a prefix integer that overflows 32 bits is a decoding error (RFC 7541 sec 5.1) ([`4b06208`](https://github.com/dstroy0/ProtoCore/commit/4b06208b37a8ae0dc41091cefaab1a1b58178863))
- the re-key count is a power of two, bounded by RFC 4253 sec 9 and RFC 4344 sec 3.2 ([`21e7af3`](https://github.com/dstroy0/ProtoCore/commit/21e7af3270ae9f48c6a1ba310ea05ee58d245c4d))
- derive the re-key packet count from the 1 GB bound (RFC 4253 sec 9) ([`fdec141`](https://github.com/dstroy0/ProtoCore/commit/fdec1417b5de819a8d703e33ea21b37d3085f962))
- a wrong KEX guess is dropped, not taken as the real KEXDH_INIT (RFC 4253 sec 7.1) ([`4f46241`](https://github.com/dstroy0/ProtoCore/commit/4f46241648f7b7344086118f54d1daf71ab9c82a))
- EXT_INFO belongs to the first NEWKEYS, not every re-key (RFC 8308 sec 2.4) ([`786a29e`](https://github.com/dstroy0/ProtoCore/commit/786a29ef6eb0a426d0e476cd8bf8d5984f0d3db3))
- the remaining ssh_dh_derive_keys_sid call sites take both directions ([`5de5ff5`](https://github.com/dstroy0/ProtoCore/commit/5de5ff53b5844c6493a9745a7006c5b211722d4f))
- native_ssh_pqc builds the SSH transport, so it has to define PC_ENABLE_SSH ([`a92ca33`](https://github.com/dstroy0/ProtoCore/commit/a92ca3346450c55f13d02ac191795a6b7de5f4c7))
- native_ssh_hardened builds the SSH stack, so it has to define PC_ENABLE_SSH ([`237d1cc`](https://github.com/dstroy0/ProtoCore/commit/237d1ccc4ab67b6ecadd9e01a31f751554d1240e))
- four audit findings - session-id binding, request fields, CR NUL, channel guards ([`9f70519`](https://github.com/dstroy0/ProtoCore/commit/9f70519f8a835e5d8c1e7e7065d8cd5825b9734b))
- refuse a password-change request instead of authing on the old password ([`d987f3e`](https://github.com/dstroy0/ProtoCore/commit/d987f3eaa7514193c7eb3500e8ff4dbf634dc522))
- end a subnegotiation only on IAC SE, not a bare 240 ([`9f18230`](https://github.com/dstroy0/ProtoCore/commit/9f18230b11caf83910e6b0b6f00a8e4b4bb273e8))
- validate the userauth service name; pin the telnet greeting length ([`f353f7b`](https://github.com/dstroy0/ProtoCore/commit/f353f7b1457948773c9da5ddbf766cc624dfdd47))
- frame a back-to-back packet pair into the slot instead of dropping the second ([`a0f9e9d`](https://github.com/dstroy0/ProtoCore/commit/a0f9e9d2d1c2a2d687651bc5516c4a32ad30cda0))
- three more blob tools onto findroot; fix sibling import for -m; guard crossinstall ([`ce7fc2c`](https://github.com/dstroy0/ProtoCore/commit/ce7fc2cb7d7c64b8c69f97a0f40e1b3b3d96a055))
- blob_parity onto findroot, and guard its module-level main() ([`54e5ca6`](https://github.com/dstroy0/ProtoCore/commit/54e5ca69856fb0b9909f27a8f08b9289cffcd437))
- base-less relpath printed CWD-relative paths; use findroot.rel ([`6f47e65`](https://github.com/dstroy0/ProtoCore/commit/6f47e6518246cbb6c12953dc6deb45f4c0f684bd))
- revive the owner-context guard, and single-source its root via findroot ([`cb99e4d`](https://github.com/dstroy0/ProtoCore/commit/cb99e4d4ebe88aa9e50b0cd3c92083f085a69fb9))
- three files took protoframe.h through log.h or telnet.h ([`0db6320`](https://github.com/dstroy0/ProtoCore/commit/0db6320ed647ef6ef0f49acd4678651de6dea44b))
- twelve PC_LOGx sites still passed their values variadically ([`a8fedec`](https://github.com/dstroy0/ProtoCore/commit/a8fedecfd4c895dd6d57453ad5b70bb5657fb575))
- the token frame passes its two strings as values ([`4d553a6`](https://github.com/dstroy0/ProtoCore/commit/4d553a623a590f7c8d08da5a4b95254599a868ee))
- a refused send ends the transfer instead of writing on ([`c9103b9`](https://github.com/dstroy0/ProtoCore/commit/c9103b91f202ff4c7e4f9794104177ccd10af5c2))
- the plaintext arena covers the SSH receive nest with compression on ([`b836619`](https://github.com/dstroy0/ProtoCore/commit/b8366194a43b9bff580f365ff84eae3a9f7cb150))
- the two PC_key_max macros get distinct names at file scope ([`929a4a0`](https://github.com/dstroy0/ProtoCore/commit/929a4a0bb94af0795e85114d873e2881bb604e9e))
- the compression buffers are wiped when the slot is reset ([`64dfb34`](https://github.com/dstroy0/ProtoCore/commit/64dfb34af65cac5cb1df008720039bf21a175adb))
- pc_ssh_conn_send reports a refused queue instead of claiming the bytes went ([`d2731b6`](https://github.com/dstroy0/ProtoCore/commit/d2731b661811a175a10f4908b320f6f1d3543c7a))
- a half-finished keyboard-interactive exchange dies with its connection ([`630f015`](https://github.com/dstroy0/ProtoCore/commit/630f015b1d205dbd73b21fa307cd3b9d91ecc70c))
- the derived session keys and the DTLS shared secret are wiped ([`7d08ead`](https://github.com/dstroy0/ProtoCore/commit/7d08ead8fa51c9ef747f80d28003e8c3dbbf84c5))
- the slot's persistent wire binds at accept, not mid-dispatch ([`1c24290`](https://github.com/dstroy0/ProtoCore/commit/1c24290703b3da4de5afc0b1bd1646f9365b4a19))
- PC_WORK_SSH_CONN carries the flight, and the crypto work it already borrowed ([`1b5fefc`](https://github.com/dstroy0/ProtoCore/commit/1b5fefca1eb6853c25f6a2c5a5a4b3456de7ec8c))
- the slot holds a flight, so the second packet of a dispatch is not dropped ([`5f251a0`](https://github.com/dstroy0/ProtoCore/commit/5f251a0e0ada4c166e385108c23d9ea903884799))
- a 2-byte over-read on the wire, two dead stdio includes, three unchecked spans ([`ba05faf`](https://github.com/dstroy0/ProtoCore/commit/ba05fafcca02a7ec2818c6622d4002bc0b10cb83))
- the scalar and the nonce do not outlive the signature ([`1414ee3`](https://github.com/dstroy0/ProtoCore/commit/1414ee36d575f61b19db9732725e3799d15c2dfc))
- the by-value transcript parameter aliased the caller's bytes ([`7591d72`](https://github.com/dstroy0/ProtoCore/commit/7591d721e587582738a806812f2c2153d0f4d9eb))
- a live context cannot share the one-shot work buffer ([`5fa288e`](https://github.com/dstroy0/ProtoCore/commit/5fa288e493c4206c386685ba33bd6a3afbef0294))
- the seed borrow is checked before it is written through ([`34a6007`](https://github.com/dstroy0/ProtoCore/commit/34a6007f74fd5fe383a4287f24ff616d84e898e6))
- the hash context is a view, so a struct copy aliases it ([`47762da`](https://github.com/dstroy0/ProtoCore/commit/47762daad43ccfb94ff2bc27546a0257fec14382))
- the key schedule needs PC_TLS13_KS_BORROW, not PC_TLS13_KS_CAP ([`b86b687`](https://github.com/dstroy0/ProtoCore/commit/b86b687d1aa1d948d2acb81f2ac2f28047feadb5))
- the slot's crypto bytes are bound before anything derives out of them ([`0a2940a`](https://github.com/dstroy0/ProtoCore/commit/0a2940a55008e1f6b1cf4106026ae1d029090c27))
- the two record layers expand their traffic keys out of the caller's bytes ([`6f1eed5`](https://github.com/dstroy0/ProtoCore/commit/6f1eed59da4a365abb4c41fb15efdf2dc1c882d8))
- the accumulator fill is the raw mover, not the aligned-span one ([`1ec2102`](https://github.com/dstroy0/ProtoCore/commit/1ec21022b996911737246a3ebc27a9b039b42555))
- the partial word merged instead of overwriting past the span ([`d62da11`](https://github.com/dstroy0/ProtoCore/commit/d62da118ee3443f643cb550f73ac368059f972ae))
- the pad and the state copy are spans, not byte walks ([`7d21790`](https://github.com/dstroy0/ProtoCore/commit/7d21790b7a3ab28b81cbb8410fd5dd5fd6d08120))
- mem.cpy stores a whole word, so the accumulator lost its length ([`5b42093`](https://github.com/dstroy0/ProtoCore/commit/5b42093eeeecbe77b432fd8b9e02fc185a23b19b))
- the key schedule leaked a persistent borrow per handshake ([`e5e180b`](https://github.com/dstroy0/ProtoCore/commit/e5e180b3b1917d7f2bacf799272cfc9d550ec3a2))
- the key schedule leaked a persistent borrow per handshake ([`3bdda91`](https://github.com/dstroy0/ProtoCore/commit/3bdda91e5621f1a8eda1027fd7716d3ca1989dd5))
- available() and read() step the open, so a polling caller advances its connect ([`fa4f91d`](https://github.com/dstroy0/ProtoCore/commit/fa4f91d3505882e05efd284b7052022e1fb14d8f))
- four vendor components become capabilities, named for what they include ([`656e8ef`](https://github.com/dstroy0/ProtoCore/commit/656e8ef8b0115b66069e5a8f599d3efa1294b8a4))
- external-RAM placement selects on PC_HAS_PSRAM ([`94ceeae`](https://github.com/dstroy0/ProtoCore/commit/94ceeae18dfbb36f7c5d14370e2e3dbbeb7d867b))
- the vendor TLS stack is a capability, not a consequence of not being the host ([`19f7c22`](https://github.com/dstroy0/ProtoCore/commit/19f7c2209d96c3af50dd0e688350a390ca8bb167))
- pc_server_reset() drops the not-found handler, and native_swar builds no library source ([`a839d8c`](https://github.com/dstroy0/ProtoCore/commit/a839d8c984777d318adb629be9905cbe8c25ddda))
- the pin guards ask for the capability, and the drivers run against the pin table ([`bfa0d4b`](https://github.com/dstroy0/ProtoCore/commit/bfa0d4b1851fea013adb65ce6292acf827061279))
- Deflate and Inflate bind their instance inside the gate that declares its type ([`e00f572`](https://github.com/dstroy0/ProtoCore/commit/e00f572ed1967de26b1c86460c93f8471b298e7f))
- the bus guards ask for the capability instead of asking about the build ([`adb5f1c`](https://github.com/dstroy0/ProtoCore/commit/adb5f1c3814f943d7f1027604710c5cf62c1fc20))
- CodeQL traces every native env, and native_codeql links again ([`e46d2f7`](https://github.com/dstroy0/ProtoCore/commit/e46d2f7ec4148a72ea1e807c468571b80c782bd2))
- fold the repeated header lines gcovr emits, which SonarQube rejects outright ([`3652741`](https://github.com/dstroy0/ProtoCore/commit/3652741f02effa245ceddb84794ea808c308e227))
- gen_compiledb and compile_examples climbed two levels to the repo root, not three ([`bd31342`](https://github.com/dstroy0/ProtoCore/commit/bd313422ae190a01b208f9756224f78e25551db6))
- select_envs resolved affected_common under the repo root, which the tools/ move broke ([`3cde0c1`](https://github.com/dstroy0/ProtoCore/commit/3cde0c185ea0f6abad835c3511a13acd5b943b65))

- Merge pull request #48 from dstroy0/mmgr-latest-ci-repair ([`b0ee534`](https://github.com/dstroy0/ProtoCore/commit/b0ee534bd170f6a9180e3ced7a142cc286ab2a8d))
- Set the PlatformIO build cache directory from HOME. ([`407dd0d`](https://github.com/dstroy0/ProtoCore/commit/407dd0dc8dfa550376fe57ffcb48ad91eab3e5bf))
- Replace the ESP32 toolchain ccache wrap with PlatformIO's build cache. ([`a76115b`](https://github.com/dstroy0/ProtoCore/commit/a76115b1d87f8801a94fe398bda488d116d0534e))
- Leave the object gcov aborts on out of its env's instrumentation and drop the sanitizer env. ([`e6056f7`](https://github.com/dstroy0/ProtoCore/commit/e6056f75f665fd7c123633f4e7d8aeb9075c13bc))
- Turn the HTTP route table on for device builds, where on_http fills it. ([`f7dbf8d`](https://github.com/dstroy0/ProtoCore/commit/f7dbf8d70f4304357c637c215d967fdce60919cf))
- Print gcovr's whole failure and run the OIDC suite under AddressSanitizer. ([`44e7797`](https://github.com/dstroy0/ProtoCore/commit/44e7797caeb8e414e306c333ba5714e2a824b004))
- Close extern C under the condition that opened it and port the ESP-IDF apps onto the current API. ([`d5a6f10`](https://github.com/dstroy0/ProtoCore/commit/d5a6f10bef439aebdc48474b038d4174ca2324fe))
- Regenerate the README quick start from the updated sketch. ([`f0bd512`](https://github.com/dstroy0/ProtoCore/commit/f0bd51228039ea26d0eae1ebbe729cd3852b5030))
- Rename the egress address helper away from the entry point it collided with. ([`e2e9ede`](https://github.com/dstroy0/ProtoCore/commit/e2e9ede33ba66e330756622b941c2348e358166f))
- Name EMBED_CALL as foreign in the naming guard. ([`cd93218`](https://github.com/dstroy0/ProtoCore/commit/cd932184ec623a80fa3302074d9396fb24123577))
- Print why gcovr produced no tracefile for an env. ([`820e39b`](https://github.com/dstroy0/ProtoCore/commit/820e39b5fcaf2be631863dcef847669ce82bd866))
- Keep the filesystem join helper's array literal in C. ([`4d498e5`](https://github.com/dstroy0/ProtoCore/commit/4d498e5a9d001dd5ad5f4b33b478047bb07b88ce))
- Make EMBED_CALL and the frame types usable from C++, and move the sketches' bring-up onto Physical's handle. ([`b841726`](https://github.com/dstroy0/ProtoCore/commit/b841726da145ce5c738a98fea7f23d0cd00f2b6e))
- Include stdatomic in the DMA host header. ([`c6c4f29`](https://github.com/dstroy0/ProtoCore/commit/c6c4f2943deffe8e4034b9ccca4c8bcd330e1c66))
- Require the driver and espcoredump components the ESP platform file includes. ([`7641b86`](https://github.com/dstroy0/ProtoCore/commit/7641b860d334ead814d6db5fe97c674d68f59d05))
- Link worker.c into the remaining envs and include the config in the DMA host header. ([`858f6ba`](https://github.com/dstroy0/ProtoCore/commit/858f6bae6e8fbbb59f0e2be65252ebccd1a8aa96))
- Require the bt component and report no release from a build without a Bluetooth controller. ([`22ca274`](https://github.com/dstroy0/ProtoCore/commit/22ca27492b67a1e33c9eb32b26d04116672a2c10))
- Link worker.c into the envs that borrow through it, accept praet's defaults for DMA builds, and match two suites to current contracts. ([`d7f0828`](https://github.com/dstroy0/ProtoCore/commit/d7f082851b0c0a694725e9309831f0bd22fff197))
- Size the UDP listener and TCP client borrows for the mmgr_ring each slot carries. ([`df0d581`](https://github.com/dstroy0/ProtoCore/commit/df0d5815575700de4b095ecc0a604550e112e700))
- Count an env that does not build in the report, refuse its coverage merge, and regenerate the README status with the report. ([`244aede`](https://github.com/dstroy0/ProtoCore/commit/244aede2492c31d726e47b685efa684eb3796cd8))
- Regenerate the README status block from the trimmed coverage report. ([`8652c30`](https://github.com/dstroy0/ProtoCore/commit/8652c304fdf0fba6ee3e886ec34536611e9f7b60))
- Prefix the atomic macros, teach the dictionary MMgr's names, and read submodule paths in the matrix guard. ([`2f1a773`](https://github.com/dstroy0/ProtoCore/commit/2f1a7738dcae5fc251293951e48538d1c383e1d6))
- Port the DMA sketches and host seam onto praet, and point the docs at MMgr's names. ([`79a95da`](https://github.com/dstroy0/ProtoCore/commit/79a95da547fe5de826eca1e17656cae82dafa5a8))
- Drop the last coverage lines past the end of the shortened number writers. ([`5484f2b`](https://github.com/dstroy0/ProtoCore/commit/5484f2b1abec9359e65396f589d4ae43e4e270ca))
